##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: person.sep_s1
    printed: "Step 1"
    label: "Enter your net profit from Schedule C (Form 1040), line 31; Schedule F (Form 1040), line 34; or Schedule K-1 (Form 1065), box 14, code A."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_sc31}
    refuses: [person.se_screen]
    shown_when: person.sep_shown
    note: "Schedule F and Schedule K-1 net earnings are outside the tool; person.se_screen covers them, so only this person's Schedule C line 31 amounts are added. Step 1 is shown under the same condition as the later steps."
  - id: person.sep_s2
    printed: "Step 2"
    label: "Enter your deduction for self-employment tax from Schedule 1 (Form 1040), line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.se_line13}
    shown_when: person.sep_shown
    note: "Schedule 1 line 15 is the return total. For this person's own worksheet, this line uses this person's Schedule SE line 13."

  # ---- Schedule C total and wage totals ----
  - id: person.h_sc31
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net profit or (loss) from Schedule C, line 31, all of this person's businesses"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31}
  - id: person.h_w2_box1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box1}
  - id: person.h_w2_box3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of Form(s) W-2, box 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box3}
  - id: person.h_w2_box7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of Form(s) W-2, box 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box7}
  - id: person.h_ss_wages
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total social security wages and tips (total of boxes 3 and 7 on Form(s) W-2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_w2_box3, person.h_w2_box7]}
    refuses: [person.rrta]
  - id: person.h_ss_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Maximum amount of wages (including tips) subject to social security tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.base}

  # ---- Form 4137 ----
  - id: person.f4137_col_c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 2: total cash and charge tips you received in 2025 (line 1, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f4137row, field: f4137row.col_c}
  - id: person.f4137_col_d
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 3: total cash and charge tips you reported to your employer(s) in 2025 (line 1, column (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f4137row, field: f4137row.col_d}
  - id: person.h_4137_line4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 4: subtract line 3 from line 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.f4137_col_c, take: person.f4137_col_d}
    note: "Form 4137 line 4 has no zero floor. The reported tips in column (d) are included in column (c), so line 4 can't be negative. The floor only guards against an inconsistent entry."
  - id: person.h_has_4137_rows
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Any Form 4137, line 1 rows (tips received from an employer to whom not all tips were reported)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.f4137_col_c, {const: 0}]}
  - id: person.f4137_under20_if_rows
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 5: cash and charge tips you received but didn't report to your employer because the total was less than $20 in a calendar month"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_has_4137_rows, {const: true}]}
          then: person.f4137_under20
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_has_4137_rows, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
  - id: person.tips_gov_if_rows
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 includes tips received as a federal, state, or local government employee whose pay was subject only to the 1.45% Medicare tax"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_has_4137_rows, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.tips_gov, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.f4137_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 6: unreported tips subject to Medicare tax. Subtract line 5 from line 4"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_4137_line4, take: person.f4137_under20_if_rows}
  - id: person.h_4137_line9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 9: subtract line 8 from line 7. If line 8 is more than line 7, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ss_base, take: person.h_ss_wages}
  - id: person.f4137_line10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 10: unreported tips subject to social security tax. Enter the smaller of line 6 or line 9"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.f4137_line6, person.h_4137_line9]}
    refuses: [person.tips_gov_if_rows, person.rrta]
    note: "The adjustment for government-employee 1.45% tips needs the amount of those tips, which the interface does not supply. That case is refused."
  - id: person.h_4137_line11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 11: multiply line 10 by 0.062"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.f4137_line10, rate: {param: f4137.rate_062}}
  - id: person.h_4137_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 12: multiply line 6 by 0.0145"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.f4137_line6, rate: {param: f4137.rate_0145}}
  - id: person.f4137_tax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137, line 13: add lines 11 and 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_4137_line11, person.h_4137_line12]}
  - id: person.f4137_filed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 4137 is filed (cash and charge tips of $20 or more in a calendar month not all reported to the employer)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.f4137_line6, {const: 0}]}
    note: "This reads 'must file' as there being unreported tips subject to tax (line 6 more than zero). Allocated tips (W-2 box 8) are outside the tool."

  # ---- Form 8919 ----
  - id: person.f8919_wages
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8919, line 6: total wages. Combine lines 1 through 5 in column (f)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8919firm, field: f8919firm.wages}
  - id: person.h_8919_line8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8919, line 8: total social security wages and tips (W-2 boxes 3 and 7) and unreported tips from Form 4137, line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_ss_wages, person.f4137_line10]}
  - id: person.h_8919_line9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8919, line 9: subtract line 8 from line 7. If line 8 is more than line 7, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ss_base, take: person.h_8919_line8}
  - id: person.h_8919_line10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8919, line 10: wages subject to social security tax. Enter the smaller of line 6 or line 9"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.f8919_wages, person.h_8919_line9]}
  - id: person.h_8919_line11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8919, line 11: multiply line 10 by 0.062"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_8919_line10, rate: {param: f8919.rate_062}}
  - id: person.h_8919_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8919, line 12: multiply line 6 by 0.0145"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.f8919_wages, rate: {param: f8919.rate_0145}}
  - id: person.f8919_tax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8919, line 13: add lines 11 and 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_8919_line11, person.h_8919_line12]}
    refuses: [person.rrta]

  # ---- Form 1040, lines 1b, 1c, 1d, 1h; Schedule 1, line 8s ----
  - id: person.household_wages_if_pay
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 1b: household employee wages not reported on Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [scope.household_pay_if_pay, {const: true}]}
          then: person.household_wages
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [scope.household_pay_if_pay, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
  - id: person.line1c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 1c: tip income not reported on line 1a (Form 4137, line 4, plus noncash tips)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_4137_line4, person.tips_noncash]}
    note: "Allocated tips in W-2 box 8 are screened elsewhere (s2.w2_marks) and are not included."
  - id: person.h_mw_biz_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medicaid waiver payments not in W-2 box 1 and not reported on Form 1099-MISC/NEC"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.mw_not_box1, take: person.mw_1099}
  - id: person.line1d
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 1d: Medicaid waiver payments not reported on Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.mw_business, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.mw_earned, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.mw_not_box1
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.mw_business, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.mw_earned, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.mw_1099
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.mw_business, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.mw_earned, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_mw_biz_earned
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.mw_business, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.mw_earned, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.zero
    note: "The inputs treat all Medicaid waiver pay as nontaxable. Without a separate business, Form 1099 amounts go on line 1d. If you choose earned income, all payments not in box 1 go there. With a home-care business, the Form 1099 amounts are reported on Schedule C instead. Open point: for a sole proprietor who chooses earned income, the remainder not on a 1099 is entered here."
  - id: person.line8s
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 1, line 8s: nontaxable amount of Medicaid waiver payments included on Form 1040, line 1a or 1d (entered as a negative number)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.mw_box1, person.line1d]}
  - id: person.mw_not_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Step 5, line 2: Medicaid waiver payments excluded on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include them in earned income (then -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.mw_earned, {const: true}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.mw_earned, {const: false}]}
          then: person.line8s
  - id: person.h_dis_wages
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable disability pensions received before minimum retirement age (reported on line 1h)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.dis_wages, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.line1h
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 1h: other earned income (strike or lockout benefits; disability pensions before minimum retirement age)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.strike, person.h_dis_wages]}
    note: "Excess elective deferrals are screened (scope.excess_deferral). Corrective distributions are not separately supplied."
  - id: person.plan_amount_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 5a: pensions and annuities, this person's Forms 1099-R"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}, {op: test, eq: [retirement.dis_wages, {const: false}]}]}
  - id: person.plan_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 5b: taxable amount of pensions and annuities, this person's Forms 1099-R"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}, {op: test, eq: [retirement.dis_wages, {const: false}]}]}

  # ---- Schedule SE ----
  - id: person.se_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 6: add lines 4c and 5b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.h_sc31, line8a: person.h_ss_wages, line8b: person.f4137_line10, line8c: person.h_8919_line10}, line: line6}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 12: self-employment tax. Add lines 10 and 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.h_sc31, line8a: person.h_ss_wages, line8b: person.f4137_line10, line8c: person.h_8919_line10}, line: line12}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 13: deduction for one-half of self-employment tax. Multiply line 12 by 50% (0.50)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.h_sc31, line8a: person.h_ss_wages, line8b: person.f4137_line10, line8c: person.h_8919_line10}, line: line13}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.h_se3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 3: combine lines 1a, 1b, and 2"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.h_sc31, line8a: person.h_ss_wages, line8b: person.f4137_line10, line8c: person.h_8919_line10}, line: line3}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen]
  - id: person.eic_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Worksheet B, line 1e (or 2c): Schedule SE line 3 (plus lines 4b and 5a) minus Schedule SE line 13"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_se3, take: person.se_line13}
    note: "When no SE tax is owed, line 13 is zero and this equals the Schedule C net profit, which is Part 2 line 2c. Optional methods and church income are screened."

  # ---- Form 2441 earned income ----
  - id: person.h_line1z
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Form 1040, line 1z (lines 1a, 1b, 1c, 1d, 1g, 1h)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_w2_box1, person.household_wages_if_pay, person.line1c, person.line1d, person.f8919_wages, person.line1h]}
  - id: person.h_dc_gross
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 1z amount plus Schedule SE, line 3"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_line1z, person.h_se3]}
  - id: person.dc_earned_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 2441, lines 18/19: earned income (line 1z, plus Schedule SE line 3 minus Schedule 1 line 15), not including dependent care benefits on line 12"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_dc_gross, take: person.se_line13}
  - id: person.dc_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 2441, lines 4/5: earned income (Form 1040 line 1z, plus Schedule SE line 3 minus Schedule 1 line 15)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.dc_earned_b}
    note: "Line 1z also includes line 1e (taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441 line 26). Line 1e is a return-level figure that depends on lines 18-19, and the text gives no per-person share. It is left out here, so this equals dc_earned_b. Clergy and statutory-employee adjustments are screened."

  # ---- Form 8880 ----
  - id: person.sav_ineligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8880: the person (a) was born after January 1, 2008; (b) is claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return; or (c) was a student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_18}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_18}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: false}]}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_student, {const: true}]}

  # ---- Schedule 1, line 16 ----
  - id: person.h_s16_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP: actual contribution, not to exceed maximum deductible contribution (step 21)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_contrib, person.sep_s21]}
  - id: person.h_s16_simple
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE IRA: salary reduction and employer contributions for yourself"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.simple_deferral, person.simple_employer]}
  - id: person.s16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 1, line 16: self-employed SEP, SIMPLE, and qualified plans (this person)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "sep"}]}
          then: person.h_s16_sep
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}
          then: person.h_s16_simple
    refuses: [person.plan_screen]
    note: "For a SIMPLE plan, contributions above the deductible limits are screened out by person.plan_screen, so the deduction is the contributions made."

  # ---- IRA Deduction Worksheet ----
  - id: person.h_ira_l2_std
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 2 amount by filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2}
  - id: person.h_ira_l2_apart
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 2: married filing separately and lived apart all year, $89,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2_apart}
  - id: person.h_ira_l2_nc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 2: married filing jointly, person not covered by a plan, $246,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2_not_covered}
  - id: person.ira_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 2 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_l2_nc
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}, {op: test, eq: [household.mfs_apart_all_year, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_l2_apart
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: false}]}]}
                - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}, {op: test, eq: [household.mfs_apart_all_year, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_l2_std
  - id: person.ira_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 6: subtract line 5 from line 2 (none deductible if line 5 is not less than line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.ira_line2, take: ira.line5}
  - id: person.h_ira_lim_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000 if under age 50 at the end of 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}
  - id: person.h_ira_lim_50
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$8,000 if age 50 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Applicable amount: $7,000 if under age 50, $8,000 if age 50 or older"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_lim_50
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_lim_base
  - id: person.h_ira_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column uses the single/head of household/MFS thresholds and rates (or is the column of a joint filer not covered by a plan)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [ira.joint_type, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_thr_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_narrow}
  - id: person.h_ira_thr_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_joint}
  - id: person.h_ira_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 amount at or above which the full amount is entered on line 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_thr_narrow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_thr_joint
  - id: person.h_ira_p70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 70%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 80%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 35%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 40%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}
  - id: person.h_ira_prod
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by the applicable percentage"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p80
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p70
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p40
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p35
  - id: person.h_ira_round
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result isn't a multiple of $10, increase it to the next multiple of $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_prod, unit: 1000, rule: up}
  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}
  - id: person.h_ira_phase
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result is $200 or more, enter the result. But if it is less than $200, enter $200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_round, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.ira_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 7 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, ge: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_threshold]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, lt: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_threshold]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_phase
    note: "Line 6 'No' (line 5 not less than line 2): none of the contributions are deductible, so this line is zero."
  - id: person.h_ira_712
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 7 or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.ira_line7, ira.line10]}
  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 12: smallest of line 7, 10, or 11 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_712, person.ira_trad]}
    refuses: [person.ira_extra_left, person.ira_recharacterized, ira.deduct_less]
  - id: person.h_ira_1011
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of IRA worksheet line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [ira.line10, person.ira_trad]}
  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 1: subtract line 12 of the IRA Deduction Worksheet from the smaller of line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_1011, take: person.ira_deduction}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 2: your total basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_basis}
    refuses: [person.ira_basis_adjusted]
  - id: person.h_8606_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 3: add lines 1 and 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_nondeductible, person.ira_8606_line2]}
  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 3 question answered Yes with a basis: distribution from a traditional IRA or Roth conversion in 2025 and line 3 more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_8606_line3, {const: 0}]}
    note: "This is read as the case where Form 8606 Part I continues past line 3 (distributions or conversions with a basis). It is true only when there is a basis to apply."
  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "File 2025 Form 8606: nondeductible contributions for 2025, or distributions/conversions with a basis more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist_gate, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_se_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net earnings from self-employment minus Schedule 1, line 15"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_sc31, take: person.se_line13}
  - id: person.ira_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 9: earned income as a self-employed individual, net earnings minus Schedule 1 lines 15 and 16; if zero or less, -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_se_a, take: person.s16}
    note: "This takes net earnings from self-employment as this person's Schedule C line 31 total. Businesses where personal services are not a material factor are screened (ira.se_capital)."
```

Second transcription's note: The inputs treat all Medicaid waiver pay as nontaxable. Without a separate business, Form 1099 amounts go on line 1d. If you choose earned income, all payments not in box 1 go there. With a home-care business, the Form 1099 amounts are reported on Schedule C instead. Open point: for a sole proprietor who chooses earned income, the remainder not on a 1099 is entered here.
